# Evidence pack: Continuum/line radiative transfer models (RADMC-3D, MCFOST, LIME, DALI) (`radiative-transfer-modelling`)

Scope: forward models of dust and line emission, synthetic observations

Built from 662 non-held-out ALMA papers whose methods cards passed mechanical evidence verification (every quote below is verbatim from the paper). Years 2012-2026.
Matcher (regex over analysis steps): `radmc|mcfost|\blime\b|\bdali\b|radiative transfer|ray[- ]?trac|synthetic (?:image|observation|cube)|simobserve|simalma`
Tagging is by regex, so a few papers may be off-topic: ignore those.

### Key parameters and settings (bibcode: value)
- 2019ApJ...881..108J: RC = 66 AU, gamma = -0.15, RC_arctan = 66 AU, gamma_arctan = 5.35, Mdust = 9.8e-5 Msun
- 2019ApJ...881..108J: Teff = 4350 K blackbody star; dust-to-gas ratio 0.001 for micron dust
- 2019ApJ...881..108J: RC fixed at 12500 AU; no exponential cutoff in gas
- 2019ApJ...881..108J: Photodissociation threshold 2.0e20 H nuclei; CO freeze-out below 20 K
- 2024A&A...687A.299L: flaring index psi = 0, scale height h_c = 0.1 at the characteristic radius
- 2024A&A...687A.299L: gas density lowered by 1e-5 inside the 15 au gas cavity and by 1e-2 between 15 and 20 au
- 2024A&A...687A.299L: grain sizes 5 nm to 1 um (small) and to 1 mm (large), MRN index -3.5
- 2024A&A...687A.299L: non-thermal desorption rates updated to 5e-4 and 1.4e-3 molecules per grain per UV photon
- 2024A&A...687A.299L: cosmic ray ionisation rate 1e-18 s^-1
- 2024A&A...687A.299L: blended components raytraced separately and summed
- 2024A&A...687A.299L: background UV 1 G0 in the fiducial model, lowered by a factor of ten in variations
- 2024A&A...687A.299L: X-ray luminosity increased by factors of 4 and 6
- 2024A&A...687A.299L: column densities smoothed with a savgol filter over 10 points, 3rd order polynomial
- 2024A&A...687A.299L: C/O = 1.01 in rings at 35-45 au and 215-225 au; fiducial C/O 0.4 elsewhere

### Limitations the authors admit
- 2019ApJ...881..108J: Simple power law underpredicts 12CO beyond ~400 AU; mass depends on the CO/H2 abundance
- 2019ApJ...881..108J: 13CO fit is not self-consistent
- 2024A&A...687A.299L: Thermochemical fits are not unique and degeneracies exist
- 2024A&A...687A.299L: Small maximum recoverable scale of the [C I] data filters emission at large radii
- 2024A&A...687A.299L: Water photodesorption is not included in the small HCO+ network, so HCO+ is overpredicted in the gap
- 2021A&A...647A.118L: Model abundances and column densities uncertain by at least a factor of a few
- 2021A&A...647A.118L: Equal HNC and HCN photodissociation rates assumed; actual HNC rate may be twice as high
- 2021A&A...647A.118L: TW Hya short-spacing coverage may affect its radial profile and apparent central component
- 2021A&A...647A.118L: Full-disk models do not represent transition-disk UV environments
- 2021A&A...647A.118L: Lupus HNC 1-0 may originate at different radii across the stacked sample
- 2021A&A...647A.118L: UV penetration and initial C and O abundances complicate a unique temperature diagnostic
- 2021A&A...647A.118L: Outer-disk lines can be subthermal; LTE inference is insufficient
- 2018A&A...612A..72J: Match is qualitative; no confidence intervals; parameters coarse; CO abundance result may be model dependent
- 2025ApJ...995L...7L: Residuals within 1 arcsec remain large, up to 50%, attributed to innermost-envelope asymmetry and the linear PV structures
- 2025ApJ...995L...7L: Simplified single power-law density and temperature contribute to residuals
- 2025ApJ...995L...7L: Efficiency of magnetic braking depends on uncertain physical parameters
- 2012ApJ...757..129R: Model parameters are not unique nor optimized; limited resolution
- 2016A&A...586A...5M: Cycle 0 lacks ACA, so extended flux is lost; mass-loss modelling degenerate
- 2016A&A...586A...5M: Distance very uncertain
- 2020ApJ...904...78S: Analytic ALMA disk masses are lower limits if the 0.87 mm emission is optically thick
- 2020ApJ...904...78S: VLA visibility fits omit the free-free contribution; models cannot fit VLA visibilities without worsening the SED fit
- 2020ApJ...904...78S: Envelope properties are poorly constrained for lack of short baselines
- 2020ApJ...904...78S: SMA C18O is contaminated by a foreground cloud
- 2016MNRAS.460.2933M: Dust mass depends strongly on assumed composition and amax; h not fully constrained
- 2026A&A...711A.159M: Extended configuration lacks short baselines and cannot properly describe structures larger than ~0.6'', filtering out up to 20% of the flux.
- 2026A&A...711A.159M: Gas densities in the very inner layers (r < r_c) are subject to important uncertainties.
- 2026A&A...711A.159M: The radiative transfer model assumes a 1D spherical envelope, whereas the observed HCN emission is asymmetric and exhibits a bright spot/clump.
- 2021MNRAS.504.2687N: LTE assumption and morpho-kinematic models are crude and oversimplified (e.g., modelling shock/pulsation broadening as Gaussian fluctuations and assuming simple power-law radial profiles).
- 2021MNRAS.504.2687N: Poor uv coverage at short baselines for the 2013.1.00166.S (28SiO, CO, HCN) data limits reliable analysis to r <= 60 au (1 arcsec) and prevents quantitative statements at larger radii.
- 2021MNRAS.504.2687N: The 3D inclination angle of the blue stream with the line of sight and the inclination of the rotation axis with the plane of the sky are unknown.
- 2016ApJ...832...37G: Missing flux relative to SCUBA-2 and a lack of more compact array data.
- 2024AJ....168..142L: Image-derived inner radius limited by focal-plane mask.
- 2024AJ....168..142L: SED inner radius and minimum grain size reach prior boundaries.
- 2024AJ....168..142L: Dust mass functions mainly as a brightness scaling factor.
- 2024AJ....168..142L: Radial surface-density profile may be biased by low back-scattering SNR or assumed scattering phase function.
- 2024AJ....168..142L: Scattered-light geometry and grain properties are degenerate.
- 2024AJ....168..142L: Surface-density assumptions can significantly change the inferred inner radius.
- 2015ApJ...812...27A: Inclination poorly constrained by the disk model; mass estimates are uncertain by optical depth, temperature, abundance
- 2015ApJ...812...27A: PV mean-position method underestimates break radius at limited resolution
- 2020NatAs...4..861C: Detailed coma morphology largely hidden by noise.
- 2020NatAs...4..861C: HCN signal-to-noise cannot distinguish a jet from isotropic outgassing.
- 2020NatAs...4..861C: Interferometric observations filter emission on angular scales ≳10''.
- 2020NatAs...4..861C: Observed coma abundances may not represent bulk nucleus volatiles.
- 2020NatAs...4..861C: Internal physical structure is poorly constrained; surface processing requires further investigation.
- 2021ApJ...909...84U: Best-fit velocity dispersion is unreliable; beam smearing from the large beam
- 2021ApJ...909...84U: Stellar surface density within ~1 kpc is poorly recovered because the HST PSF HWHM is comparable to that scale
- 2021ApJ...909...84U: CO(5-4) flux differs from the earlier Seko16 measurement because of velocity width and data reduction differences
- 2024A&A...685A.142Y: Static background omits emission inside ISCO and variability.
- 2024A&A...685A.142Y: Exponential temperature evolution is not a physical synchrotron-cooling model.
- 2024A&A...685A.142Y: Spot shearing and interactions with the background are omitted.
- 2024A&A...685A.142Y: Negative spins excluded; orbital period only weakly depends on spin.
- 2024A&A...685A.142Y: Background fit has limited inference power and remains dissociated from the spot component.
- 2020MNRAS.498.1319M: Planet mass assumes the gap is empty at its centre; a shallower, broader gap would change the inferred width and mass
- 2020MNRAS.498.1319M: Frankenstein profiles lack a primary beam correction
- 2020MNRAS.498.1319M: Lower S/N makes gap width and depth comparison with other discs difficult
- 2020MNRAS.498.1319M: Derived planet masses must be taken with caution since the gap may be inherited or the planet may have migrated
- 2025PSJ.....6..286M: ALMA rubidium-maser cable-routing error likely introduced an unknown frequency shift and slight spectral broadening
- 2025PSJ.....6..286M: ALMA winds are not vertically resolved; equatorial-jet contributions cannot be isolated
- 2025PSJ.....6..286M: GCM and ALMA forward-model inputs differ in vertical treatment
- 2025PSJ.....6..286M: Best combined fit is driven by the poorer-fitting egress lightspot; untested wind shapes may fit better
- 2025PSJ.....6..286M: Longitudinal symmetry excludes possible localized atmospheric events
- 2025PSJ.....6..286M: HASI lacks temporal and latitudinal resolution
- 2025PSJ.....6..286M: Omitting methane overestimates bending angles by roughly 0.5% under the stated 1.5% methane assumption
- 2025PSJ.....6..286M: Mid-event arcs are not reproduced by any tested forward model
- 2021MNRAS.503.1459B: The torus-to-SMBH mass ratio is fixed to M_torus/M_smbh = 0.02 rather than varied as a free parameter.
- 2021MNRAS.503.1459B: Simulations assume a dissipationless system and neglect cloud collisions and tidal shearing/merging.
- 2021MNRAS.503.1459B: Radiation pressure on cloud dynamics near the accretion disk is neglected.
- 2021MNRAS.503.1459B: Temperature distribution model uses a simple blackbody equilibrium approximation and neglects radiative transfer in a dusty medium within the torus.
- 2026A&A...706A.174A: Stellar mass and other SED-derived properties highly uncertain because of Lupus-I extinction
- 2026A&A...706A.174A: Lens redshift not confirmed; MUSE and FORS2 data too poor
- 2026A&A...706A.174A: Dust mass strongly depends on the assumed kappa and optical depth
- 2026A&A...706A.174A: Unresolved data and fixed GDMR and [CO]/[H2] prevent tight gas-mass constraints
- 2026A&A...706A.174A: A minor AGN contribution cannot be ruled out
- 2017Icar..286..289L: Lower mesosphere and stratopause temperatures are more uncertain, and the HCN vertical distribution is not unique given moderate optical thickness
- 2017Icar..286..289L: Spatial resolution insufficient to resolve surface
- 2025A&A...699A..70H: H2CO abundances and parameters are poorly constrained
- 2025A&A...699A..70H: Derived EAR depends on the choice of CH3OH collisional rates
- 2025A&A...699A..70H: Very high H2CO temperatures in GMC 4 and 6 are not well constrained
- 2021A&A...647A..10G: Bad weather limited SOFIA/GREAT observations to ~70 min on a single flight, so the achieved rms (50 mK) is insufficient for an unambiguous detection of SH, providing only upper limits.
- 2021A&A...647A..10G: Specific inelastic collision rate coefficients for SH with H and H2 are not available, requiring mass-scaling from SH-He rates without hyperfine resolution.
- 2021A&A...647A..10G: Gas-grain models do not include formation of more complex S-bearing ices (s-OCS, s-H2S2, s-S_n, s-SO2, s-HSO) or gas-phase reactions of S+ with desorbed organic molecules like H2CO, and assume steady…
- 2021A&A...647A..10G: Low-temperature (< 1000 K) radiative and dielectronic recombination rates of S+ used in PDR models may not be sufficiently accurate.
- 2015ApJ...814..143A: Smooth spherical shell model does not reproduce fine structure; infrared pumping only partly included
- 2017A&A...604A...2S: Resolution too poor to separate the two nuclei
- 2025A&A...700A.141H: Rest frequencies of the NHD2 lines are probably less accurate than those of the NH2D lines
- 2025A&A...700A.141H: Static core model neglects core contraction during chemical evolution
- 2025A&A...700A.141H: Fit uncertainties include only statistical noise
- 2022FrASS...9.1118D: The genetic algorithm is purely a mathematical solution and does not verify whether the atmospheric plasma is in local thermodynamic equilibrium (LTE).
- 2022FrASS...9.1118D: Low spatial resolution of ALMA single-dish observations (25" at 230 GHz, 58" at 100 GHz) smooths the dark umbra (25" diameter) with surrounding bright areas, masking the umbra at 100 GHz and reducing…
- 2022FrASS...9.1118D: The modeled atmospheric profiles that fit 230 and 100 GHz well cannot reach the high brightness temperatures observed at 17 GHz in non-polarized regions (reaching at most 1.2 T_qS vs. 2.8 T_qS observ…
- 2022FrASS...9.1118D: The modeled 17 GHz polarized gyroresonance source is smaller and rounder than the observed elliptical source.
- 2024A&A...691A..70N: Expansion velocity is a lower limit because of projection effects
- 2024A&A...691A..70N: Three-dimensional geometry unknown; effects of clustered SNe not well understood; comprehensive kinematic analysis left to future work
- 2015ApJ...806...17R: Warm-gas mass per nucleus uses global Herschel ratio
- 2019MNRAS.485.2457H: Coarse spectral resolution (3.4 km/s) may overestimate dispersions and Mach number; Mach number is an upper bound
- 2019MNRAS.485.2457H: Cloud line-of-sight extent unknown; PPV not equivalent to physical space
- 2020PASJ...72...33K: Equation-of-state limit is weak
- 2020PASJ...72...33K: Absolute values of the X-ray pseudo pressure are arbitrary
- 2020PASJ...72...33K: Temperature at r > 250 kpc cannot be constrained by the ALMA data
- 2020PASJ...72...33K: X-ray-only deprojection includes statistical errors only
- 2019ApJ...875L...5E: Thermal single-parameter electron model, untilted disks, nonradiative GRMHD, fast-light approximation
- 2015ApJ...812...43B: Resolution may cause over-predicted sizes and under-predicted magnifications; optically faint lenses may be missed
- 2016PASJ...68...88K: Missing flux on large scales due to no Total Power Array
- 2013ApJ...772..149C: Only time-steady heating modeled; no flares, nonthermal emission, or 3D geometry
- 2020ApJ...894...76W: gamma Vir models lack constraints at other wavelengths; temperature minimum cannot be analyzed
- 2020ApJ...894...76W: Variability at longer timescales cannot be ruled out for gamma Lep
- 2020ApJS..251...14H: Completeness assumes point sources and does not capture extended emission; masses uncertain by factor ~2
- 2024A&A...682A.127S: Spatially unresolved asymmetry and only marginal resolution of the molecular remnant.
- 2024A&A...682A.127S: Overlapping components and moderate/high optical depths undermine detailed component fits.
- 2024A&A...682A.127S: CASSIS saturation correction is limited.
- 2024A&A...682A.127S: CO may have been ejected before the merger, changing the expansion-time assumption; paper describes the distance as an upper limit.
- 2024A&A...682A.127S: Atomic shock models cannot constrain density or shock velocity.
- 2024A&A...682A.127S: H2 models do not constrain external UV or cosmic-ray ionisation; density limit reaches the model boundary.
- 2024A&A...682A.127S: Reliable rotational and vibrational temperatures unavailable for VO, CrO, and ScO.
- 2024A&A...682A.127S: 2022 optical physical-condition analysis not attempted because extinction makes constraints increasingly difficult.
- 2024NatAs...8..765L: Recovery is highly ill-posed and not unique; relies on the imposed gravitational and synchrotron models
- 2024NatAs...8..765L: Error bars show stability of local optima, not posterior uncertainty
- 2024NatAs...8..765L: Un-modelled accretion-disk variability affects recovery because the model attributes all dynamics to orbital motion
- 2024A&A...689A..67G: Single-dish observations lack the spatial resolution to resolve the BCG from the CGM tail, requiring fixed kinematic priors from ALMA and a point-source approximation at 4'' offset to correct for bea…
- 2024A&A...689A..67G: Single-dish detections have low S/N (e.g. S/N = 1.2 for the tail [CI](2-1) component and S/N = 2.4 for BCG CO(7-6)) due to atmospheric absorption at ~595-600 GHz (APEX) and low elevation ~30 deg (NRO…
